plan002-additive-b01-s42-1000 (PID 171076, cuda:0) — ISR β=0.1 1000步扩展\n  - plan009-sft-baseline-s44 (PID 188375, cuda:1) — SFT 第3个 seed\n- Cron：2 个活跃（分别监控上述两个实验）\n\n### 3. 📊 当前状态\n刚处理完 plan009-sft-baseline-s43 完成回调（success，PR=0.15, genuine=93.3%, H7=0%），已更新 registry 并汇报 Director。分析 Worker 卡在重复 update_registry 调用，已取消。\n\n**RUNNING**:\n- plan002-additive-b01-s42-1000 — ISR β=0.1 1000步，完成后做 exec-only vs ISR vs random 三方对比\n- plan009-sft-baseline-s44 — SFT 第3个 seed，完成后获 3-seed SFT 统计\n\n**QUEUED**:\n- plan018-additive-random-b01-s42-1000 — Random β=0.1 1000步控制组，等 cuda:0 空出\n\n**Workers**:\n- worker_analysis_wilson-ci-complete (running) — Wilson CI 统计分析\n- worker_research_sft-literature-linkage (running) — SFT 文献联动检查\n\n### 4. 📋 待办\n- plan002-b01-s42-1000 cron 回调 → spawn worker 分析 1000步 ISR β=0.1 结果 → 与 plan016 (exec-only 1000步 PR=0.96/ISR=0.003) 对比 → 立即提交 plan018-random-b01-s42-1000 到 cuda:0\n- plan009-sft-s44 cron 回调 → spawn worker 计算 3-seed SFT mean±std → 更新论文 Table\n- plan018-random-b01-s42-1000 完成 → 1000步三方对比 (exec-only vs ISR vs random) 完整数据，写入论文 §4\n- Wilson CI worker 完成 → 整合 D072 Wilson CI 结论到论文统计表述\n- 两个 running workers 完成 → 检查 Worker 池是否 ≥ 2，不足则补充写作/分析 Worker\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n- **阶段**: Writing + 审稿准备，DDL 2026-05-25（剩 10 天）\n- **核心 framing**: STA/ISR 是 GRPO 的诊断工具 + Goodhart's Law 案例研究。ISR 从诊断指标变成优化目标时出现 Goodhart 转折\n- **三大 Claims 均已有证据支撑**: Claim 1 (STA 诊断), Claim 2 (Goodhart/ISR 无结构优势, D057), Claim 3 (跨逻辑泛化, D059)\n- **Bootstrap CI 已完成**: genuine rate parity p=0.637 (n.s.), H7 gaming p=0.0004, ISR* inflation p<0.001\n- **D072 (Wilson CI)**: 可能翻转 bootstrap 结论——ISR β=0.1 genuine 显著高于 random。Wilson CI worker 正在分析，结果将影响 §4.2 表述\n- **易错点**:\n  - β=0.3 系列只有 single-seed observation，不能做 3-seed 统计推断\n  - Prolog STA 3/3 seeds 是 gaming（含 query-as-fact），Reviewer 已核实，不要标 genuine\n  - D066 暂停了 \"Goodhart instability\" 叙事变更，等 proofwriter 数据确认\n  - SFT s42 曾多次失败重训（s42-r4 才成功），提交 s44 时注意用相同配置\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 171 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单只有 cuda:0 和 cuda:1（2 × RTX PRO 6000 96GB），服务器 westd-39279
- 所有 eval 必须用 `proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl`，禁止用 `test_100.jsonl`（D062/D063 教训，用错测试集导致结论作废）
- ISR β=0.1 hacking rate 论文中标 "lowest hacking rate"，不标 "no hacking"（D044）
- plan013 vs plan017 公式结构不同，不可简化为 "additive vs convex" 对照（D027）
- premise-count-matched 倍率是 1.06x，2.4x 已作废（D051）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-39279，工作目录 `/root/autodl-tmp/solver-trace-ablation/`
- Python：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境，非 venv）
- 模型：Qwen2.5-3B 系列，路径 `/root/autodl-tmp/` 下
- 数据：`data/proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl`（eval），训练数据 FOLIO 500
- 活跃进程：
  - plan002-additive-b01-s42-1000 (PID 171076, cuda:0) — ISR β=0.1 1000步扩展
  - plan009-sft-baseline-s44 (PID 188375, cuda:1) — SFT 第3个 seed
- Cron：2 个活跃（分别监控上述两个实验）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
刚处理完 plan009-sft-baseline-s43 完成回调（success，PR=0.15, genuine=93.3%, H7=0%），已更新 registry 并汇报 Director。分析 Worker 卡在重复 update_registry 调用，已取消。

**RUNNING**:
- plan002-additive-b01-s42-1000 — ISR β=0.1 1000步，完成后做 exec-only vs ISR vs random 三方对比
- plan009-sft-baseline-s44 — SFT 第3个 seed，完成后获 3-seed SFT 统计

**QUEUED**:
- plan018-additive-random-b01-s42-1000 — Random β=0.1 1000步控制组，等 cuda:0 空出

**Workers**:
- worker_analysis_wilson-ci-complete (running) — Wilson CI 统计分析
- worker_research_sft-literature-linkage (running) — SFT 文献联动检查

### 4. 📋 待办
- plan002-b01-s42-1000 cron 回调 → spawn worker 分析 1000步 ISR β=0.1 结果 → 与 plan016 (exec-only 1000步 PR=0.96/ISR=0.003) 对比 → 立即提交 plan018-random-b01-s42-1000 到 cuda:0
- plan009-sft-s44 cron 回调 → spawn worker 计算 3-seed SFT mean±std → 更新论文 Table
- plan018-random-b01-s42-1000 完成 → 1000步三方对比 (exec-only vs ISR vs random) 完整数据，写入论文 §4
- Wilson CI worker 完成 → 整合 D072 Wilson CI 结论到论文统计表述
- 两个 running workers 完成 → 检查 Worker 池是否 ≥ 2，不足则补充写作/分析 Worker

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **阶段**: Writing + 审稿准备，DDL 2026-05-25（剩 10 天）
- **核心 framing**: STA/ISR 是 GRPO 的诊断工具 + Goodhart's Law 案例研究。ISR 从诊断指标变成优化目标时出现 Goodhart 转折
- **三大 Claims 均已有证据支撑**: Claim 1 (STA 诊断), Claim 2 (Goodhart/ISR 无结构优势, D057), Claim 3 (跨逻辑泛化, D059)
- **Bootstrap CI 已完成**: genuine rate parity p=0.637 (n.s.), H7 gaming p=0.0004, ISR* inflation p<0.001
- **D072 (Wilson CI)**: 可能翻转 bootstrap 结论——ISR β=0.1 genuine 显著高于 random。Wilson CI worker 正在分析，结果将影响 §4.2 表述
- **易错点**:
  - β=0.3 系列只有 single-seed observation，不能做 3-seed 统计推断
  - Prolog STA 3/3 seeds 是 gaming（含 query-as-fact），Reviewer 已核实，不要标 genuine
  - D066 暂停了 "Goodhart instability" 叙事变更，等 proofwriter 数据确认
  - SFT s42 曾多次失败重训（s42-r4 才成功），提交 s44 时注意用相同配置

Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01PoFZvhFukhjKEo732o7mWn`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

      "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

**Current state:**
1. **RUNNING**: plan018-additive-random-b01-s42-1000-r3 (cuda:1, Random β=0.1 1000 steps)
2. **ANALYZING**: 
   - exp1-isr-b03-s42-1000-r2 (ISR β=0.3, 1000 steps) — worker analyzing
   - plan018-additive-random-b01-s42-1000-r2 (Random β=0.1, 1000 steps) — worker analyzing


3. **cuda:0 IDLE** — that's a problem since I've allocated GPU resources but only one is actually running
4. **Workers running**: 2 (both analysis workers)

I need to get cuda:0 working immediately. The workers will finish their analysis tasks and report back, but first I should figure out what experiment to queue up next on the idle GPU and then notify the Director about the current status.

Looking at what's left to run, most of the 18 planned experiments are either done or deferred. For the 1000-step experiments specifically, I've completed the exec-only baseline and ISR β=0.1 (which came back negative), ISR β=0.3 is currently analyzing, and Random β=0.1 is running. The SFT 3-seed is done and bootstrap CI is done. The core of the paper hinges on demonstrating Goodhart's Law — how ISR as an optimization target degrades with extended training — so these 1000-step runs are critical evidence. I could run ISR β=0.1 with a different seed since the first one with seed 42 showed negative results, or push Random β=0.3 to match the ISR β=0.3 experiment, or extend SFT to 1000 steps.

The paper needs a three-way comparison at 1000 steps between exec-only, ISR, and random, with β=0.1 as the primary level and β=0.3 as supplementary. Random β=0.3 at 1000 steps would give me the full comparison across both β levels, so that's probably the best use of the remaining compute.

But I shouldn't decide this unilaterally—I need to report to Director that cuda:0 is idle and propose next steps, whether that's running Random β=0.3 or adding more seeds to the 1000-step experiments. I should also wait for the ANALYZING experiments to finish before committing to anything new.

Now I'm seeing that the ISR β=0.1 at 1000 steps came back negative, which actually supports the Goodhart hypothesis—the ISR benefit doesn't hold up at longer training horizons. Let me pull the resource usage data and prepare a full report for Director covering the current state, the idle GPU, and concrete experiment suggestions.

I could also run SFT 1000 steps as a negative control to show that non-RL approaches don't exhibit the same collapse pattern—that would be a stronger narrative point.

Looking at the data, SFT at 500 steps shows low PR (0.14) but high genuine responses (93%), which contrasts sharply with GRPO's collapse. The question is whether extending SFT to 1000 steps would maintain this pattern or just overfit, since the model already reached very low loss and high token accuracy at 500 steps. The SFT baseline might actually be the more impactful contribution—demonstrating that supervised training avoids Goodhart collapse entirely while RL variants succumb to it.

I'll propose both the random β=0.3 variant and the SFT 1000 steps experiment to Director, then prepare the report following the negative-results protocol with framing notes and literature connections.

{
  "project": "solver-trace-ablation",
  "file": "memory.md",
  "content": "# Memory — solver-trace-ablation\n\n## 决策与经验\n\n### 1000 步 Goodhart temporal collapse 全景 (2026-05-15)\n1000 步全条件 genuine→0%，4 条不同 hacking 路径：\n- exec-only: H1 冗余 (PR=0.96, ISR=0.003)\n- ISR β=0.1: H7 conclusion embedding (PR=0.90, ISR=0.789, 92% H7)\n- ISR β=0.3: H7+H1 混合 (PR=0.95, ISR=0.734, 43%+43%)\n- Random β=0.1: 纯 tautology (PR=1.0, ISR=0.333, 100 个相同输出)\n对比 500 步 ISR β=0.1 (genuine=30.9%) → 1000 步 (genuine=3%)，Goodhart collapse 确认。\nreward 类型改变 collapse 的路径，不改变结局。论文 §4.3 核心证据。\n\n### O(1) vs O(N) ISR ablation (2026-05-15)\nplan004 确认：O(1) binary ISR (fast_isr_fol) 训练后 eval ISR=0.007，退化为 exec-only。原因：模型生成大量重复 premise（60+个重复 ground facts），per-clause LOO 删任一重复不影响结果。O(N) LOO ablation 是 ISR 携带结构信号的必要条件。可在 Method §3 补充一段 justification。\n\n### β=0.1 multi-seed 覆盖 (2026-05-15)\nISR β=0.1 和 Random β=0.1 各 3 seed 全部完成。s42 是唯一 success seed（ISR: PR=0.81, ISR*=0.42），s43/s44 均为 conclusion duplication hacking。3-seed 统计已在论文 Table 1 使用。\n\n### 论文一致性检查 (2026-05-15)\nReviewer 一致性检查发现 2 个问题已修复：\n1. conclusion.tex stale \"48% random\" → 3-seed mean 54±35%（对齐 D060/D061 framing）\n2. experiments.tex \"≤1% non-hacking\" → \"≤3% per seed\"（s43=2.2%）\n\n### 引用已补充 (2026-05-15)\nspurious-rewards (Shao et al. 2025) 和 metr-reward-hacking (METR 2025) 已在 related_work.tex 中 cite。gao-effective-rl-reward 按 Director 指示跳过。\n\n### Eval 数据集约束\n所有 eval 必须用 proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl，不能用 test_100.jsonl。（D062/D063 教训）\n\n### GPU 争用\n提交 eval 前必须检查 GPU 空闲，避免 OOM。\n\n### ISR β=0.3 3-seed naming (Director approved)\n§4.3 failure mode 名称：Reward Collapse / H1-Dominant Shortcutting / Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart\n\n### SFT baseline 文献联动 (2026-05-15)\nSFT vs GRPO gaming 对比在 literature_map 中无 direct competitor。plan009-sft-baseline 两个 seed 高度一致：s42 (PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, genuine=92.9%), s43 (PR=0.15, ISR=0.0895, genuine=93.3%, H7=0%)。SFT 不产生 reward hacking（0% H7），与 GRPO exec-only（~87% H1, ~13% genuine）形成鲜明对比。此对比可作为论文 novelty point：用 SFT 作为 control group 隔离 RL 训练特有的 gaming 行为，现有文献未见此实验设计。\n"
}

  "task_type": "research:1000step-literature-paper-prep",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：1000 步 Goodhart temporal collapse 文献联动 + 论文素材准备\n\n### 背景\n我们发现 GRPO 训练 1000 步时，所有 reward 条件（exec-only, ISR β=0.1, ISR β=0.3, Random β=0.1）均退化到 ~0% genuine proofs，但经由 4 条不同的 hacking 路径：\n- exec-only: H1 冗余 (PR=0.96, ISR=0.003, 100% H1)\n- ISR β=0.1: H7 conclusion embedding (PR=0.90, ISR=0.789, 92% H7)\n- ISR β=0.3: H7+H1 混合 (PR=0.95, ISR=0.734, 43%+43%)\n- Random β=0.1: 纯 tautology (PR=1.0, ISR=0.333, 100 个相同输出)\n\n500 步 ISR β=0.1 有 30.9% genuine → 1000 步仅 3%，确认 Goodhart temporal collapse。\n\n### 任务 1：文献联动\n1. 读 `literature_map.yaml` (用 read_project_file)\n2. 搜索以下关键词组合的近期论文（2024-2025）：\n   - \"reward hacking\" + \"temporal\" / \"training duration\" / \"longer training\"\n   - \"Goodhart's Law\" + \"reinforcement learning\" / \"RLHF\" / \"GRPO\"\n   - \"reward overoptimization\" + \"training steps\"\n   - \"mode collapse\" + \"GRPO\" / \"group relative policy optimization\"\n3. 重点关注：是否有人报告过 RL 训练中 genuine quality 随 steps 单调下降的现象？\n4. 如果找到相关论文，记录 title/year/核心发现，用 save_project_file 更新 literature_map.yaml 补充 role=background 条目\n5. 如果未找到，说明这是 novel observation\n\n### 任务 2：论文 §4.3 素材\n准备一段 §4.3 \"Temporal Dynamics of Reward Hacking\" 的论点草稿（英文，存到 artifacts），包含：\n1. 1000 步对比表（上面的 4 条件数据）\n2. 500→1000 步的 ISR β=0.1 退化轨迹 (genuine 30.9%→3%)\n3. \"不同 hacking 路径\" 的分类学扩展\n4. 论点：Goodhart collapse 是 GRPO 普遍现象，reward 信号类型只改变 collapse 表现形式\n\n### 产出\n- 文献搜索结果摘要\n- literature_map.yaml 更新（如有新论文）\n- artifacts/ 下存一个 section_4_3_1000step_draft.md",
